DARTree first constructs a fixed-width candidate tree by expanding and scoring all nodes at each depth in a single batch, and then only applies best-first pruning to select the verification tree, decoupling AR-head inference from sequential heap operations. Across seven math, code, and chat benchmarks, DARTree achieves the highest average acceptance length and speedup in all four model--temperature configurations, accepting up to 12.97 tokens per verification round, 98.6\% more than DFlash and 27.9\% more than Domino in the same setting, and reaching up to 9.73$\times$ lossless speedup over locally measured autoregressive decoding.

</details>


### [91] [Exponential Convex Calibration Dimension for the Multi-Label Jaccard Measure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13549)
*Mingyuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明每实例Jaccard分数（IoU）的损失矩阵关于2^s个结果是可逆的，并给出了精确校准的CC维数界2^{s-1} ≤ CCdim ≤ 2^s-1，表明零遗憾校准需要指数级的预测坐标。同时提出两个多项式维度的近似保证：F1-to-Jaccard转换和基于MinHash的平方损失代理，使得任意固定加性遗憾容差都能在多项式预测维度下实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Jaccard分数（IoU）是多标签分类和二元分割中的标准评估指标，但其校准性（calibration）和代理损失设计缺乏严格的理论分析。作者希望理解Jaccard损失的几何结构，并回答一个核心问题：要实现校准的代理损失，预测维度需要多大？这关乎实际应用中代理损失设计的可行性与效率。

Method: 本文采用三重方法：(1)结合有限MinHash Gram表示与Boolean Möbius反演，证明Jaccard分数、shifted-loss和普通损失矩阵的非奇异性及损失列仿射维度为2^s-1；(2)通过因子加权分布和Bayes最优报告构造下界证明CCdim的下界2^{s-1}；(3)提出两种多项式维度的近似方案：一个F1-to-Jaccard遗憾转换（将现有F1代理转换为Jaccard代理）以及基于MinHash平方损失的直接构造及其有符号变体。

Result: 主要结果：(1)证明了Jaccard相关损失矩阵的可逆性和仿射维度2^s-1；(2)证明精确校准的CC维数界为2^{s-1} ≤ CCdim(L^Jac) ≤ 2^s-1，从而任何精确校准的凸代理需要指数多预测坐标；(3)获得F1-to-Jaccard转换，给出渐近Jaccard遗憾上界3-2√2；(4)MinHash平方损失代理在任意条件标签分布上均匀达到Jaccard遗憾容差α，直接构造维度为O((s^2+s·log(1/ρ))/α^2)，有符号变体维度为O((s+log(1/ρ))/α^2)。

Conclusion: 结论揭示了一个根本性的维度权衡：零遗憾校准需要指数预测维度，而任何固定的加性遗憾容差都只需多项式预测维度。这意味着在实际应用中（允许有限遗憾），多项式维度的代理损失是可行的，但追求完美校准则不可行。这为理解和设计Jaccard指标的代理损失提供了理论框架。

Abstract: The per-instance Jaccard score, or intersection over union (IoU), is standard in multi-label classification and binary segmentation. With $s$ labels, its loss matrix has $2^s$ outcomes and reports. Under the convention $\mathrm{Jac}(\varnothing,\varnothing)=1$, we prove that the Jaccard score, shifted-loss, and ordinary loss matrices are nonsingular and that the loss columns have affine dimension $2^s-1$. The proof combines a finite MinHash Gram representation with Boolean Möbius inversion. For exact calibration, we prove $2^{s-1} \leq \mathrm{CCdim}(L^{\mathrm{Jac}}) \leq 2^s-1$. The lower bound uses a factorially weighted distribution with $2^{s-1}+1$ supported outcomes and Bayes-optimal reports. Consequently, every exactly calibrated convex surrogate requires exponentially many prediction coordinates. We also give two polynomial-dimensional approximation guarantees with explicit regret transfers. A new $F_1$-to-Jaccard transfer turns an existing $(s^2+1)$-dimensional $F_1$ surrogate into a polynomial-time rule with asymptotic Jaccard regret at most $3-2\sqrt{2}$. For any $α>0$ and $0<ρ<1$, a MinHash square-loss surrogate attains Jaccard-regret floor $α$ uniformly over arbitrary conditional label distributions. With probability at least $1-ρ$, the direct construction has dimension $O((s^2+s\log(1/ρ))/α^2)$, while a signed variant has dimension $O((s+\log(1/ρ))/α^2)$. Thus zero-regret calibration requires exponential dimension, whereas every fixed additive regret tolerance admits polynomial prediction dimension.

</details>


### [92] [Defensive Boosting for Online Probabilistic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13554)
*Georgy Noarov,Aaron Roth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Defensive Booster，一个统一的在线概率预测算法，同时获得两类通常由不同在线boosting技术分别提供的保证：Brier分数上与span of H竞争（在线梯度提升）以及分类误差在弱学习条件下趋于零（在线弱到强提升）。该算法通过实现boosting的"对偶视角"来运作，仅需访问一个弱类别学习器，效率远高于先前方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的在线boosting技术分别提供两种不可比较的保证：在线梯度提升在Brier分数上与H的span竞争，但若span中无准确预测器则无保证；在线弱到强提升在弱学习条件下驱动分类误差为零，但条件不满足时几乎无承诺。需要一个单一的算法同时获得这两种保证。

Method: 提出Defensive Booster算法，其关键在于将mistake weights作为对偶变量：当算法的随机分类误差持续偏高时，权重形成一个平滑重加权分布，使得所有弱假设在此分布上的edge都较低，从而得到ex-post hard-core证书证明弱学习条件不成立。若条件成立则分类误差降低，若条件不成立则Brier分数仍有竞争力。还开发了强自适应变体，在每个时间区间上同时满足两种保证。

Result: Defensive Booster在每条自适应序列上，Brier分数与在线梯度提升以相同速率竞争于span of H；同时当实际transcript满足平滑弱学习条件时，Brier分数和随机分类误差与在线分类提升具有相同速率保证。算法极为高效，仅需一个弱类别学习器，而先前方法需要维护大规模弱学习器集成。实验表明预测性能强（有时大幅超越所有基线），运行速度快数个数量级。

Conclusion: Defensive Booster实现了在线预测中"防守型"与"进攻型"保证的统一，通过boosting的对偶视角自然融合了两种现有方法的优势，提供了理论完备且实际高效的解决方案，并对强自适应场景同样适用。

Abstract: We study online probabilistic forecasting of binary outcomes chosen by an adaptive adversary. Given an online learning algorithm for a weak hypothesis class $H$, we would like to efficiently obtain two incomparable guarantees that existing online boosting techniques provide separately. Online gradient boosting competes in Brier score with the best predictor induced by the span of $H$ on every sequence, but promises nothing when the span does not contain an accurate predictor. Online weak-to-strong boosting drives classification error to zero under a weak-learning condition, but promises little when that condition fails.
  We give a simple defensive forecasting algorithm, the Defensive Booster, that obtains both guarantees. On every adaptive sequence, its Brier score is competitive with the best prediction induced by the span of $H$ at the same rate as online gradient boosting; simultaneously, whenever the realized transcript satisfies the smooth weak-learning condition, its Brier score and randomized classification error satisfy the same rate guarantee as online classification boosting. This is achieved by operationalizing the "dual view" of boosting: When the algorithm's randomized classification error is persistently high, its mistake weights form a smooth reweighting on which every weak hypothesis has low edge, yielding an ex-post hard-core certificate that the weak-learning condition fails. We also develop a strongly adaptive variant, which satisfies both guarantees on every time interval. The Defensive Booster is very efficient: it accesses just one weak-class learner, whereas the prior online boosting methods we compare against maintain large weak-learner ensembles. Experiments on synthetic and real data streams demonstrate its strong predictive performance (sometimes substantially improving over all prior baselines) coupled with orders-of-magnitude faster runtime.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [93] [Offering Microsecond-Scale Cross-VM Core Elasticity on Colocated Lightweight Virtual Machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12633)
*Yibo Yan,Seo Jin Park*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: HyperFlux是一个基于KVM的超轻量级VM底层架构，使VM的并行度（物理核心数）在运行时弹性可调，能在13微秒内跨VM移动核心（比vCPU热插拔快几个数量级），同时保持3.2MB内存占用和1.37ms冷启动时间，在混合部署场景下相比Firecracker和Cloud Hypervisor可降低高优先级VM尾延迟最多10倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Serverless平台通过在同一基础设施上混合部署大量快速启动、内存精简的VM来提高部署密度。为每个VM过度配置以满足峰值需求可以保护流量突发期间的尾延迟，但会损害部署密度。现有的VM底层架构都无法在微秒级时间尺度上动态转移物理核心：传统VM的vCPU热插拔需毫秒级，Firecracker在启动时固定核心数，最快的超轻量VM则完全放弃多核执行。这使得在保持高密度的同时有效保护尾延迟成为一个未解决的难题。

Method: HyperFlux是一个基于商业化KVM的超轻量VM底层架构，核心创新在于使VM的并行宽度（支撑它的物理核心数）在运行时具有弹性。它能够将一个物理核心从一个繁忙的捐赠者VM强占并快速转移给另一个VM，整个过程仅需13微秒。

Result: HyperFlux可以将核心跨VM转移仅需13微秒（即使从繁忙的捐赠者VM强制回收），比vCPU热插拔快几个数量级；VM内存占用仅3.2MB，冷启动仅1.37ms，与最快的超轻量VM相当，同时独特地支持多核并行。在混合部署下，高负载时相比Firecracker和Cloud Hypervisor的静态核心共享，可降低高优先级VM尾延迟最多10倍；与cgroup和vCPU热插拔相比，在变化的负载突发下提供更低且更稳定的尾延迟。

Conclusion: HyperFlux证明了在保持超轻量VM快速启动和极小内存占用优势的同时，可以实现微秒级、弹性可变的并行度，从而在高密度混合部署场景下有效保护尾延迟，填补了现有VM底层架构在微秒级核心迁移能力上的空白。

Abstract: Serverless platforms commonly colocate many diverse workloads, each in a fast-booting, memory-lean virtual machine (VM), to improve deployment density. Overprovisioning each VM for its peak protects tail latency during traffic bursts but hurts density; maintaining high density while effectively protecting tail latency requires the infrastructure to be able to shift physical cores, at a microsecond timescale, to whichever latency-sensitive VM is bursting and reclaim them as the burst subsides. No VM substrate delivers this: conventional VMs resize a guest's cores only through a millisecond-scale vCPU hot-plug path, Firecracker fixes a VM's core count at boot, and the ultralight VMs that boot fastest drop multicore execution entirely.
  We present HyperFlux, a commodity-KVM ultralight VM substrate that makes a VM's parallelism width (the number of physical cores backing it) elastic at runtime. We show that HyperFlux can move a core across VMs in merely 13$μ$s, even when forcibly reclaiming it from a busy donor, orders of magnitude faster than vCPU hot-plug. A HyperFlux VM incurs only a 3.2MB memory footprint and can cold-boot in 1.37ms, on par with the fastest-booting ultralight VMs, while uniquely supporting multicore parallelism. Under colocation, it can reduce high-priority VMs' tail latency by up to 10x under high load compared to static core-sharing with Firecracker and Cloud Hypervisor, and deliver a lower and more stable tail latency compared to using cgroup and vCPU hot-plug under changing load bursts.

</details>


### [94] [InFactPlanner: Planning Sustainable Geo-Distributed LLM Data Centers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12915)
*Nicoletta Tsiopani,Moysis Symeonides,George Pallis,Marios D. Dikaiakos*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出InFactPlanner，一个基于trace驱动的决策支持框架，用于LLM推理场景下可持续AI数据中心部署的假设分析（what-if analysis），支持单站点和地理分布式站点，综合评估能耗、碳排放、水消耗、延迟和服务利用率，帮助运营商在基础设施大规模建设前比较不同部署方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理的快速增长使得可持续性关注从一次性训练转向持续服务阶段，基础设施决策直接影响能源使用、碳排放、水消耗和服务质量。然而运营商在构建大规模基础设施前需要比较不同部署方案，直接测量成本高、速度慢且有时不可行，缺乏有效的决策支持工具。

Method: InFactPlanner结合查询trace、硬件模型配置、候选站点配置、PUE/WUE参数、可再生发电模型和随时间变化的电网碳强度，估算功率、能耗、碳排放、水使用、延迟和服务器利用率。框架将底层服务效应抽象为可配置的硬件模型配置文件，支持快速比较站点选择、容量部署、硬件、模型、可再生能源整合和路由选择。

Result: 能量核算流程与参考LLM推理能量估算相比偏差小于10%；框架支持多数据中心和不同服务器数量的可扩展性评估；展示了硬件选择、可再生能源部署、地理部署和碳感知路由等场景驱动决策分析。结果表明可持续性最优选择可能与延迟最优选择不同，且部署的碳价值强烈依赖于本地电网结构。

Conclusion: InFactPlanner为可持续AI数据中心部署提供了有效的决策支持，揭示了可持续性目标与传统性能目标（如延迟）之间可能存在冲突，并强调了本地电网碳强度对部署碳价值的关键影响，为运营商在基础设施部署决策中平衡可持续性与服务质量提供了工具和方法。

Abstract: The rapid growth of LLM inference is shifting sustainability concerns from one-time training to continuous serving, where infrastructure decisions shape energy use, carbon emissions, water consumption, and service quality. Yet operators often need to compare deployment alternatives before large-scale infrastructure is built, making direct measurement costly, slow, and sometimes infeasible. We present InFactPlanner, a trace-driven decision-support framework for what-if analysis of sustainable AI data center deployment for LLM inference across single and geo-distributed sites. InFactPlanner combines query traces, hardware-model profiles, candidate site configurations, PUE/WUE parameters, renewable generation models, and time-varying grid carbon intensity to estimate power, energy, carbon emissions, water use, latency, and server utilization. The framework abstracts low-level serving effects into configurable hardware-model profiles, enabling rapid comparison of site selection, capacity placement, hardware, model, renewable integration, and routing choices. We validate the energy accounting pipeline by reproducing reference LLM inference energy estimates with less than 10% deviation, evaluate scalability across multiple data centers and server counts, and demonstrate scenario-driven decision analyses for hardware selection, renewable placement, geographic deployment, and carbon-aware routing. Our results show that sustainability-optimal choices can differ from latency-optimal ones, and that the carbon value of deployment depends strongly on the local grid mix.

</details>


### [95] [Efficient Randomized LL/SC that Preserves History Independence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12946)
*Dante Bencivenga,Homa Habashi,Philipp Woelfel*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于随机化的线性化LL/SC对象实现，在n个进程系统中达到常数期望步复杂度，空间复杂度为O(nτ+m)，相较于最优确定性算法的Ω(n²τ+m)空间界限有显著改进，并适用于quiescently history-independent（QHI）算法，可实现在可用硬件上高效运行动态哈希算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LL/SC（Load-Link/Store-Conditional）是并发编程中的基本同步原语，但在实际硬件中通常不可用，需要软件实现。已有最优确定性算法需要Ω(n²τ+m)个基础对象（CAS和寄存器），且此前没有可比的随机化算法。此外，STOC 2025上提出的QHI动态哈希算法使用了Θ(m)个硬件LL/SC对象，但硬件并不提供LL/SC，需要一种具有类似性质的软件实现来将该算法落实在实际硬件上。

Method: 通过结合随机化以及FADD（Fetch-and-Add）原语（配合CAS和寄存器），在弱自适应对手模型下设计了一个新的LL/SC对象实现。核心思路是利用随机化来打破确定性算法中由于进程间竞争导致的空间开销瓶颈，将空间复杂度从Ω(n²τ+m)降低到O(nτ+m)，同时保持常数期望步复杂度。

Result: 获得了O(nτ+m)的空间复杂度，对抗弱自适应对手。当m=O(1)时，该结果与仅使用CAS和寄存器的算法下界匹配。此外，该实现支持QHI性质，能够在无挂起操作且无未完成LL操作时使内部内存状态仅由m个LL/SC对象的值唯一确定。在m=Ω(n)的合理假设下，可无渐近步复杂度和空间复杂度损失地在现有硬件上实现STOC 2025的QHI动态哈希算法。

Conclusion: 本文结果表明，通过随机化以及FADD等基础原语的组合，可以显著改善LL/SC软件实现的空间效率，并且使原本依赖于硬件LL/SC的高级算法（如QHI动态哈希）能够在实际可用硬件上以相同的渐近复杂度运行，弥合了理论算法与硬件可用性之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: We study the fundamental problem of implementing $m$ linearizable LL/SC objects with constant expected step complexity in a system of $n$ processes, using bounded base objects commonly available in hardware. Assuming that each process may have at most $τ$ outstanding LL operations, the best known deterministic algorithm requires $Ω(n^2τ+ m)$ base objects (CAS and registers) [Blelloch and Wei, DISC 2020]. Previously, no comparable randomized algorithm was known.
  By employing randomization and FADD in addition to CAS and registers, we obtain a space bound of $O(nτ+m)$ against the weak adaptive adversary. For $m=O(1)$ this matches a lower bound for algorithms using CAS and registers [Aghazadeh and Woelfel, PODC 2015].
  In addition, our object can be employed by quiescently history-independent (QHI) algorithms: Whenever no operation on the object is pending and no process has an outstanding LL operation, its internal memory state is uniquely determined by the values of the $m$ LL/SC objects. An important application is a QHI dynamic hashing algorithm presented at STOC 2025, which uses $Θ(m)$ hardware LL/SC objects to maintain a hash table of size $m$ [Attiya, Bender, Farach-Colton, Oshman, and Schiller, STOC 2025]. But LL/SC is not available in hardware, and prior to our work no wait-free or efficiently lock-free software implementation of LL/SC with similar properties was known. Our work demonstrates that one can actually implement the hashing algorithm on available hardware, without an asymptotic increase in step and space complexity, under the reasonable assumption that $m=Ω(n)$.

</details>


### [96] [TEMPO: Makespan-Aware Expert-Parallel Load Balancing Across Memory- and Compute-Bound Regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13057)
*Jie Li,Chenxin Jia,Jinliang Shen,Cunzhuang Liu,Ruiyi Ding,Jianwen Xian,Kang He,Chengru Song*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文指出现有专家并行（EP）MoE 服务中的负载均衡调度器（如 EPLB、LPLB、UltraEP、METRO）基于"专家耗时与token数或激活专家数线性相关"的假设，但真实测量表明耗时是分段非线性的：在阈值约156-168 tokens以下受HBM权重流主导（成本与激活副本而非token挂钩），之上则受分组GEMM的128-tile填充影响（拆分专家增加填充计算）。作者提出max-affine时间模型 t=max(a+bG, c+βN) 捕获两种状态，形式化逐批调度为带固定费用的makespan问题（在双GPU全复制下NP难），并设计了makespan感知调度器，在8-GPU和端到端测试中验证其性能，核心主张是"相图预测"而非"普适赢家"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EP MoE调度器假设专家执行时间与token数或激活专家数线性相关，但实际测量表明这一假设不成立——存在HBM权重流主导区和分组GEMM填充区两个不同的计算状态。这导致现有代理指标在真实负载下产生1.4-1.6倍的建模块时间偏差（p95达1.7倍），且不同代理指标会在不同状态下翻转胜负。需要一个可以精确建模实际执行时间并据此调度的方案。

Method: 1) 通过两台不同代际数据中心的GPU实测，发现EP MoE专家执行时间呈两段式非线性：低于阈值token数时由HBM权重流主导（成本绑定激活副本数），高于阈值时分组GEMM将M维度按128-tile取整（拆分专家会增加填充计算）。2) 提出max-affine时间模型 t=max(a+bG, c+βN)（G为激活副本，N为token数）同时刻画两种状态。3) 将逐批调度形式化为带固定费用的makespan问题，证明在双GPU完全复制下为NP难，退化极限下多项式可解。4) 设计makespan感知调度器，在关键路径外毫秒级求解，SGLang集成采用进程外运行并将调度与计数收集融合为单图内kernel。

Result: 在8-GPU Testbed A微基准上，调度器在所有场景下与最佳固定基线差距在1%以内，在状态混合场景下最多提升15.5%。端到端Testbed B上，Qwen3-235B（位于赢区）吞吐量提升4-6%，p99延迟降低约15.6%；DeepSeek-V3（位于通信主导区）仅体现机制开销，无显著收益。这验证了"相图预测"的核心主张——系统能预先预测部署是在赢区还是非赢区。

Conclusion: 论文主张的核心贡献不是"普适的性能提升"，而是提出了一个基于真实执行剖析的相图框架：它能在部署前预测特定模型/硬件组合下makespan感知调度是否有效。在混合状态下（同时存在热专家处于线性区、冷专家处于平坦区）收益显著；在通信主导场景下则没有额外收益。这种刻意限定的主张范围增强了工作可信度，为MoE调度研究提供了更精确的执行时间模型。

Abstract: In expert-parallel (EP) MoE serving, every layer synchronizes at the slowest GPU. Dispatchers balance token counts (EPLB, LPLB, UltraEP) or activated-expert counts (METRO), assuming expert time is linear in one. Measurements on two datacenter GPU generations show it is neither: below $\nstar\!\approx\!156$--$168$ tokens, HBM weight streaming dominates---cost attaches to \emph{activated replicas}, not tokens; above it, grouped GEMM rounds tokens to 128-tile $M$-tiles, so \emph{splitting} an expert adds padded compute. A max-affine profile $t=\max(a+bG,\,c+βN)$ captures both regimes. Realistic decode batches hold hot experts in the linear regime and cold in the flat \emph{simultaneously}; recorded batches show proxy dispatches differ by $1.4$--$1.6\times$ in modeled block time (p95 up to $1.7\times$), and \emph{which} proxy wins flips with the regime. We formalize per-batch dispatch as a fixed-charge makespan problem---NP-hard on two fully replicated GPUs, polynomial in degenerate limits---and present \sys{}, a makespan-aware dispatcher solving it in milliseconds off the critical path; its SGLang integration runs out-of-process and fuses dispatch with count collection into one in-graph kernel. Anchored by an 8-GPU Testbed~A microbenchmark, \sys{} stays within 1\% of the best fixed baseline everywhere and wins by up to $15.5\%$ where regimes mix. End-to-end on Testbed~B, Qwen3-235B (inside the win region) gains $4$--$6\%$ throughput and cuts p99 latency by ${\sim}15.6\%$; DeepSeek-V3 (outside, communication-dominated) shows only mechanism cost. A phase diagram, not a universal win, is the claim: it predicts both outcomes before deployment.

</details>


### [97] [Meshlib: In-Process Policy Enforcement for Sidecar-less Service Meshes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13107)
*Habib Mostafaei,Tom van Liempd*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Meshlib 是一个基于 Cilium 的无 sidecar 服务网格扩展，通过应用内库强制执行 L7 策略，同时利用 Cilium 的 eBPF 数据面处理传输层身份与路由，消除 sidecar 引入的延迟和资源开销，在 TrainTicket 基准上实现了最低端到端延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有服务网格依赖每 pod 的 sidecar 代理，在每个请求上进行冗余的应用层解析，导致显著的延迟和资源开销。如何在消除 sidecar 的同时不牺牲安全保证是核心挑战。

Method: Meshlib 采用职责分离架构：非侵入性的应用绑定库在应用进程内强制执行 L7 策略，而传输层身份认证和路由则委托给 Cilium 的 eBPF 数据面处理。该架构与未修改的服务完全互操作，支持在现有部署中增量采用。

Result: 在 TrainTicket 基准上对 37 个服务强制执行 126 条安全策略，与 Istio、Linkerd 和未修改的 Cilium 相比，Meshlib 在所有评估配置中实现了最低的端到端延迟，且资源开销相当。

Conclusion: 通过将 L7 策略执行下沉到应用进程内并将传输层功能交给 eBPF 数据面，Meshlib 成功消除了 sidecar 带来的延迟开销，同时保持了服务网格的安全语义，且不影响与现有服务的互操作性。

Abstract: Service meshes facilitate service-to-service communication and enforce security policies in microservice architectures. However, they often depend on per-pod sidecar proxies, which introduce significant latency and resource overhead due to redundant application-layer parsing on every request. Eliminating sidecars without compromising security guarantees remains a central challenge. To address this, we introduce Meshlib, a sidecar-less service mesh extension built on Cilium as a control-plane extension. Meshlib incorporates a non-intrusive application-bound library that enforces Layer-7 policies within the application process, while delegating transport-level identity and routing to Cilium's eBPF-based data plane. This separation of responsibilities removes sidecar-induced latency and maintains the security semantics of the service mesh. The architecture remains fully interoperable with unmodified services, enabling incremental adoption within existing deployments. We evaluate Meshlib against Istio, Linkerd, and unmodified Cilium on the TrainTicket benchmark, enforcing 126 security policies across 37 services and show that it achieves the lowest end-to-end latency of all evaluated configurations with comparable resource overhead.

</details>


### [98] [Validation-Centric AI-Assisted GPU Porting of a 250,000+ Line Legacy Weather Simulation Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13122)
*Tetsuya Hoshino,Masaya Kato,Kazuhisa Tsuboki,Daichi Mukunoki,Takahiro Katagiri,Toshihiro Hanawa*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文通过CReSS（一个超过25万行代码的Fortran气象模拟程序）案例研究，提出了一种以验证为中心的AI辅助GPU移植工作流。该工作流利用AI代理提取OpenMP区域、生成基于转储的内核基准、应用OpenACC变换，并通过逐元素比较和应用程序级验证来保证科学有效性。在真实台风模拟中，该工作流为162个目标内核生成了经过数值验证的GPU实现，实现了5.1倍的应用级加速，同时检测出5个内核中的数值差异（由浮点和内建函数差异引起），并提炼出会话上下文管理、运行时状态重建和小规模静态分析遗漏恢复等关键挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型遗留科学应用（如CReSS）是经过长期发展积累信用的科学资产，其GPU移植必须在适配GPU中心化HPC系统的同时保持科学有效性。现有AI辅助移植方法过于关注代码生成，忽视了验证这一关键环节，因此需要一种以验证为中心的工作流来确保移植后的数值结果与原始实现一致。

Method: 提出一种验证中心化的AI辅助GPU移植工作流，包含以下步骤：1）AI代理提取OpenMP区域；2）从物理上有意义的模拟状态生成基于转储（dump）的内核基准；3）应用OpenACC变换；4）通过与转储参考数据的逐元素比较和应用程序级验证来验证结果。工作流设计特别关注会话跨越上下文管理、运行时状态重建和静态分析遗漏恢复。

Result: 在真实台风模拟中，工作流为162个目标内核生成了数值验证的GPU实现，取得了5.1倍的应用级加速，且开发成本在实用的墙钟时间范围内。此外，检测到5个内核中的数值差异，这些差异由浮点和内建函数差异引起，包括阈值敏感的分支发散和消去效应，并将此反馈给应用开发者。

Conclusion: 大型遗留科学应用的AI辅助GPU移植不仅需要代码生成能力，更需要验证中心化的工作流设计。会话跨度的上下文管理、运行时状态重建以及小规模静态分析遗漏的昂贵恢复是实际应用中的关键挑战，这为未来AI辅助HPC移植工具的设计提供了重要指导。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models have made CLI-based AI agents a practical tool for accelerating GPU porting of large legacy scientific applications. Such applications, however, are not merely old code bases; they are scientific assets whose credibility has been accumulated through long-term development, comparison with observations, and use in domain studies. GPU porting must therefore preserve this scientific validity while adapting the implementation to GPU-centric HPC systems. This paper presents a validation-centric AI-assisted GPU porting workflow through a case study of CReSS, a legacy Fortran weather simulation code with more than 250,000 lines. The workflow uses an AI agent to extract OpenMP regions, generate dump-based kernel benchmarks from physically meaningful simulation states, apply OpenACC transformations, and validate results through element-wise comparison with dumped reference data and application-level validation. Using a real typhoon simulation, the workflow produced numerically validated GPU implementations for 162 target kernels and achieved a 5.1x application-level speedup within practical wall-clock development cost. In particular, it detected numerical discrepancies in five kernels caused by floating-point and intrinsic-function differences, including threshold-sensitive branch divergence and cancellation effects, enabling feedback to the application developers. The case study suggests that, for large legacy scientific applications requiring dump-based validation, practical AI-assisted GPU porting must manage session-spanning context, runtime-state reconstruction, and costly recovery from small static-analysis omissions. These findings demonstrate that AI-assisted GPU porting requires not only code generation, but validation-centric workflow design.

</details>


### [99] [LipCache: A Local Inference Proxy with Certified Caching for Edge Image Classification Service](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13144)
*Zhengzhe Xiang,Yinlin Chen,Fuli Ying,Binbin Zhou,Hailiang Zhao,Schahram Dustdar*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出LipCache，一个面向边缘图像分类的可认证语义缓存框架。通过引入受Lipschitz约束的轻量网络GuardNet，为每个样本计算基于分类边际和谱范数的认证重用半径，将缓存命中从经验阈值判断转变为具有明确理论边界的几何认证决策，在CIFAR、Tiny-ImageNet和SVHN上实现最高1.65倍加速，同时保证所有缓存命中满足认证一致性条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义缓存方法依赖经验相似度阈值来决定缓存重用，虽然能提高命中率，但在分类决策边界附近容易引入静默错误分类（silent misclassifications），缺乏可靠的理论保障。随着边缘侧视觉服务向低延迟、高吞吐方向发展，需要在降低推理成本的同时不牺牲可靠性，因此亟需一种具有可认证保证的缓存机制。

Method: LipCache框架包含以下核心组件：(1) 不修改现有主模型MainNet，仅在其旁侧引入轻量网络GuardNet，将输入映射到低维特征空间并施加Lipschitz约束；(2) 从局部分类边际和分类头的谱范数出发，为每个样本计算一个逐样本的认证重用半径（certified reuse radius）；(3) 运行时，仅当查询特征落入已缓存结果的认证重用球内时才重用缓存结果，否则回退到MainNet；(4) 将缓存命中从经验阈值测试转化为带有明确理论边界的几何认证决策；(5) 进一步提出增强的GuardNet训练策略以提高小样本类别上的缓存命中率。

Result: 在CIFAR、Tiny-ImageNet和SVHN等标准图像分类任务上，LipCache实现最高1.65倍的测量加速比，端到端精度损失有限；所有被接受的缓存命中均满足GuardNet侧的认证一致性条件。增强的GuardNet训练方法在Tiny-ImageNet多类扩展上显著提升缓存命中率，同时维持100%的认证一致性率。

Conclusion: 逐样本的可认证重用能够在保证理论一致性的前提下减少主模型回退次数，为边缘侧的可靠缓存辅助推理提供了一条可行路径。LipCache证明，通过将缓存重用决策从经验判断转变为具有明确数学边界的认证决策，可以在提升推理效率的同时维持可靠性的可验证保证。

Abstract: As edge-side vision services continue to expand toward low-latency, high-throughput scenarios, reducing the inference cost of vision models without sacrificing reliability has become a central concern. Existing semantic caching methods largely rely on empirical similarity thresholds; while such thresholds improve hit rates, they tend to introduce silent misclassifications near decision boundaries. To address this, we propose \texttt{LipCache}, a certified semantic caching framework for image classification. Without modifying the existing deployed main model, \texttt{MainNet}, the framework introduces a lightweight network, \texttt{GuardNet}, that maps inputs into a low-dimensional feature space subject to a Lipschitz constraint. It then computes a per-sample certified reuse radius from the local classification margin and the spectral norm of the classification head. At runtime, a cached result is reused only when the query feature falls inside the certified reuse ball; otherwise, the query falls back to \texttt{MainNet}. Thus, cache hits are transformed from empirical threshold tests into geometric certification decisions with explicit theoretical boundaries. Across standard image classification tasks like CIFAR, Tiny-ImageNet, and SVHN, \texttt{LipCache} achieves a measured speedup of up to $1.65\times$ with limited end-to-end accuracy degradation, while all accepted cache hits satisfy the \texttt{GuardNet}-side certified-consistency condition. Furthermore, an enhanced \texttt{GuardNet} training recipe substantially improves cache hit rates in the Tiny-ImageNet multi-class extension while maintaining a certified-consistency rate of $100\%$. These results demonstrate that per-sample certified reuse can reduce main-model fallback while preserving theoretical consistency, providing a feasible approach to reliable cache-assisted inference at the edge.

</details>


### [100] [Triangle-Free Coloring in LOCAL via Resilient Lovász Local Lemma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13357)
*Peter Davies-Peck,Xusheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文改进了Pettie和Su的分布式三角形无图着色算法，通过采用Davies的"弹性"（resilience）定义，将算法中Lovász局部引理（LLL）实例的求解复杂度降低到log^{O(1)}log n轮，从而使LLL不再是算法的瓶颈，最终获得了o(Δ)颜色下首个log^{O(1)}log n轮的三角形无图着色算法，以及匹配最优存在性上界的girth-5着色算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三角形无图着色是sublogarithmic随机分布式算法技术的重要基准问题。现有最优算法（Pettie-Su）虽然只需O(k+log^* n)次LLL应用，但分布式LLL本身仍是困难问题，现有最快算法需要O(log_Δ n)或O(Δ/log Δ)+log^{O(1)}log n轮，这成为算法的瓶颈。作者希望消除这一瓶颈，获得更快的三角形无图着色算法。

Method: 作者改编Pettie-Su算法，采用Davies [SODA 2023]提出的"弹性"（resilience）定义来构造LLL实例，使得这些实例可以在log^{O(1)}log n轮内求解。当k=log^{ω(1)}log n时不需要LLL步骤，因此整体复杂度为O(k)+log^{O(1)}log n轮。该框架同样适用于girth-5图，使用(1+ε)Δ/ln Δ种颜色。

Result: 获得了首个在log^{O(1)}log n轮内用o(Δ)种颜色对三角形无图着色的算法；同时获得了girth-5图的着色算法，使用(1+ε)Δ/ln Δ种颜色，在O(k)+log^{O(1)}log n轮内完成，匹配已知的最佳存在性上界。

Conclusion: 通过结合Davies的弹性定义，LLL步骤不再是分布式三角形无图着色算法的瓶颈，实现了将LLL求解轮数压缩到log^{O(1)}log n的目标，为后续sublogarithmic分布式算法提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: The Lovász Local Lemma (LLL) is a probabilistic tool that has been shown to be of central importance in the study of distributed algorithms. For example, the constructive LLL is known to be complete for the class of locally-checkable labeling problems with $o(\log n)$ randomized complexities in the LOCAL model. One classic application of the LLL is in coloring graphs with some sparse structure, such as triangle-free graphs. Triangle-free coloring therefore serves as a benchmark problem for techniques for sublogarithmic randomized distributed algorithms. The state-of-the-art distributed triangle-free coloring algorithm of Pettie and Su [ICALP 2013, Information and Computation 2015] uses $\fracΔ{k}$ colors (where $k$ can be up to $(\frac14 - \varepsilon)\ln Δ$) and consists of $O(k+\log^* n)$ applications of the distributed LLL. However, the distributed LLL is itself a difficult problem; despite significant study, the fastest algorithms known require $O(\log_Δn)$ or $O(\fracΔ{\logΔ})+\log^{O(1)}\log n$ rounds. In this work, we adapt the Pettie-Su's algorithm so that the resulting LLL instances can be solved in $\log^{O(1)}\log n$ rounds, by employing the 'resilience' definition of Davies [SODA 2023]. This gives an $O(k)+ \log^{O(1)}\log n$ complexity (since the LLL is not needed when $k= \log^{ω(1)}\log n$), essentially causing the LLL steps to no longer be the bottleneck of the algorithm. As a corollary we obtain the first $\log^{O(1)}\log n$-round algorithms for coloring triangle-free graphs with $o(Δ)$ colors. The same framework also yields a companion girth-$5$ algorithm, using $(1+\varepsilon)Δ/\ln Δ$ colors in $O(k)+ \log^{O(1)}\log n$ rounds, matching the best known existential upper bound for the number of colors.

</details>


### [101] [Fast Tendermint: Speeding Up a Foundational Consensus Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13434)
*Preston Vander Vos,Daniel Cason*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Fast Tendermint是Tendermint共识协议在n>5f条件下的改进版本，将良好情况下的决策延迟从三个通信步骤缩短到两个，同时保留Tendermint的领导者轮换结构，并提供了形式化的正确性证明和Quint规范。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低延迟区块链的需求推动了共识协议优化的研究。Tendermint作为广泛研究和部署的BFT共识协议，在部分同步模型下需要三个通信步骤才能达成共识，而近期的研究表明当拜占庭进程数少于n/5时可以缩短为两个通信步骤。

Method: Fast Tendermint将Tendermint的prevote和precommit步骤合并为单一投票步骤，并将locked和valid状态合并，从而在保持Tendermint领导者轮换机制的同时将良好情况下的决策步骤从三缩短为二。作者提供了协议的形式化规范，使用Quint语言（一种TLA+的现代语法）进行模型检验。

Result: Fast Tendermint在n>5f条件下能够在两个通信步骤内达成共识，并给出了agreement、validity和termination的形式化证明，同时通过Quint规范进行了模型检验验证。

Conclusion: Fast Tendermint成功地将Tendermint适配到n>5f设置，在保留原始协议核心结构（包括领导者轮换）的同时显著降低了决策延迟，为低延迟区块链提供了有效的共识方案。

Abstract: Tendermint is among the most widely studied and deployed Byzantine fault-tolerant (BFT) consensus protocols, owing in part to its native leader-rotation mechanism that subsumes complex view changes. Like most partially-synchronous BFT protocols, Tendermint tolerates $f < n/3$ Byzantine processes and decides in three communication steps. Motivated by the push for lower-latency blockchains, a recent line of work shows that consensus can be solved in two communication steps when $f < n/5$. We present Fast Tendermint, an adaptation of Tendermint to the $n > 5f$ setting that decides in two communication steps in the good case, while preserving Tendermint's leader-rotation structure. Fast Tendermint collapses Tendermint's prevote and precommit steps into a single voting step and merges the $locked$ and $valid$ state. We give proofs of agreement, validity, and termination, and a formal specification in Quint, a modern surface syntax for TLA+, used to model-check the protocol.

</details>


### [102] [OpScale: Operator-level Provisioning and Autoscaling for LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13499)
*Xingqi Cui,Chieh-Jan Mike Liang,Ziang Tang,Jiarong Xing,Haoran Qiu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出OpScale，一个以算子为粒度的LLM推理服务弹性伸缩编排框架，通过算子级弹性替代传统的整模型粒度伸缩，在满足SLO的前提下显著降低GPU资源消耗和功耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM推理服务的自动伸缩以整个模型为最小伸缩单元，粒度太粗，无法捕捉推理负载的细粒度动态变化，导致突发流量下SLO违约或GPU利用率低下。作者通过特征分析发现推理工作负载中的算子具有显著的异构性，因此算子级弹性是一个可行的伸缩原语。

Method: OpScale是一个算子级别的编排框架，包含四个核心组件：1) profiling（算子特征剖析）、2) provisioning（资源供给）、3) placement（算子放置）、4) runtime serving（运行时服务）。为应对算子级粒度带来的高复杂度和空间爆炸问题，OpScale设计了相应的简化与优化策略。

Result: 在多达40块A100和24块GB200上使用生产环境trace进行评测，OpScale在满足SLO的前提下最多可节省36.3%的GPU数量和28%的功耗，或在固定成本预算下实现44%的吞吐量提升。

Conclusion: 算子级弹性是比整模型粒度的伸缩更高效的资源管理方式，OpScale验证了该方向的可行性，能够同时兼顾SLO保证与成本效率。

Abstract: Achieving cost efficiency while meeting strict user-facing SLOs (e.g., time-to-first-token) remains a fundamental challenge for cloud GPU clusters serving large language models (LLMs). Autoscaling is the key mechanism for cluster resource management, yet a basic system design question is open for serving LLMs: what should be the unit of scaling? Existing approaches primarily treat the entire model as a monolithic scaling unit--simple but unable to capture the fine-grained dynamics of inference workloads. As a result, such coarse-grained scaling often leads to either SLO violations under bursty demand or significant GPU under-utilization.
  Our characterization reveals substantial operator heterogeneity, exposing operator-level elasticity as a viable scaling primitive. We present OpScale, a practical operator-level orchestration framework of profiling, provisioning, placement, and runtime serving. OpScale is designed to tackle the high complexity and the space explosion problem, arising from operating at this finer granularity. Evaluated with production traces on up to 40 A100s and 24 GB200s, OpScale attains SLOs with up to 36.3% fewer GPUs and 28% less power, or achieves 44% higher throughput under fixed cost budgets.

</details>


### [103] [RoutePack: Expert Placement and Attention-Aware Data Packing for MoE Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12146)
*Yibo Shen,Xudong Han,Xiaowei Zhu,Gen Li,Zhenxuan Pan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: RoutePack是一个面向MoE强化学习训练的分层规划器，通过状态一致的逐层专家重路由和联合注意力/专家感知的数据打包，将训练吞吐量提升8.85%–14.89%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型的RL训练面临两个耦合的负载均衡问题：序列组合决定数据并行微批次中的密集注意力工作量，而token路由决定专家并行rank上的稀疏专家工作量。单独优化任一者会导致瓶颈转移到另一侧，需要联合优化。

Method: RoutePack包含两个核心阶段：(1) 逐层专家重路由——利用rollout阶段路由回放中每个样本的序列长度和逐层专家需求，独立地在各MoE层按聚合路由需求放置专家；(2) 路由感知数据打包——将样本打包到最小的可认证（或已知最优可行）数量的token上限执行行中，并通过投影EDP-shard感知目标优化DP布局。该目标结合窗口归一化的线性-二次注意力代理与逐层物理EP-rank峰值，最小化最慢EDP分片的累积代价。并行种群退火搜索保持固定数量的可行布局，同时保证样本覆盖、容量、非空单元、微批次数量相等和通信拓扑一致。状态一致物化保持逻辑top-k路由和现有MoE内核，无需微批次级专家复制。

Result: 在Ling-3.0-Tiny和Ling-3.0-Flash上，专家重路由分别提升平均训练器测量的token吞吐量3.80%和10.50%，路由感知打包额外增加4.86%和3.98%，总体吞吐量较基线提升8.85%和14.89%。

Conclusion: RoutePack通过联合优化专家路由与数据打包，有效解决了MoE RL训练中注意力与专家工作负载之间的瓶颈转移问题，验证了两个维度的协同优化优于单独优化，且完全兼容现有MoE内核。

Abstract: Training Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models for reinforcement learning (RL) couples two load-balancing problems: sequence composition determines dense attention work in each data-parallel microbatch, while token routing determines sparse expert work on expert-parallel ranks. Optimizing either alone can shift the bottleneck to the other. In MoE RL, rollout-time routing replay exposes every sample's sequence length and layer-wise expert demand before its training step. We present RoutePack, a hierarchical planner that coordinates state-consistent, layer-wise expert rerouting with joint attention- and expert-aware data packing over an optimizer-step window. RoutePack first places experts independently at each MoE layer using aggregate routing demand. It then packs samples into the smallest certified, or best-known feasible, number of token-capped execution rows and optimizes their DP layout with a projected EDP-shard-aware objective. The objective combines a window-normalized linear-quadratic attention proxy with per-layer physical EP-rank peaks and minimizes the accumulated cost of the slowest EDP shard. Parallel population annealing searches fixed-row feasible layouts while preserving sample coverage, capacity, nonempty cells, equal microbatch counts, and communicator topology. State-consistent materialization preserves logical top-k routing and existing MoE kernels without microbatch-level expert replication. Across Ling-3.0-Tiny and Ling-3.0-Flash, expert rerouting improves mean trainer-measured token throughput by 3.80% and 10.50%, while routing-aware packing adds another 4.86% and 3.98%, respectively. Overall, RoutePack improves throughput by 8.85% and 14.89% over the baseline.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [104] [Position: Reasoning is a Learnable Rule-Based Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12325)
*Rachel Lawrence,Jacqueline Maasch*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇立场论文（position paper），指出生成式AI社区在"推理"（reasoning）定义上缺乏一致的操作性定义，导致推理评估的构念效度（construct validity）无法验证，阻碍了可信自主推理的可量化进展。作者提出基于文献综合的操作性定义（将有效且可靠的推理定位为可学习的基于规则的过程），并提供了一套AI推理研究传播的最佳实践清单。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理是AI领域最具科学和经济价值的方向之一，但生成式AI社区未能在推理的操作性定义上达成共识，且往往隐式排斥逻辑与可验证自动推理的历史性处理方式。这种定义上的模糊性使得推理评估的构念效度无法验证，进而削弱了通往可信自主推理的可量化进展。

Method: 作者采用立场论证的方式，首先指出现有定义模糊性的问题及其后果，然后基于文献综合提出操作性定义，将有效（valid）且可靠（sound）的推理定位为一种可学习的基于规则（rule-based）的过程；最后构建了一个用于AI推理研究传播的最佳实践检查清单。

Result: 论文产出了两项成果：（1）一套基于文献综合的操作性定义，将推理重新定位为可学习的规则化过程；（2）一份最佳实践检查清单，用于规范AI推理研究的沟通与报告方式。

Conclusion: 作者认为定义模糊性是可以通过达成共识来解决的。通过提供操作性定义和传播规范清单，本文旨在使推理评估的构念效度变得可验证，从而促进向可信自主推理的量化进展。

Abstract: Autonomous reasoning is among the most scientifically and economically motivating topics in AI today. Historically the purview of symbolic AI, recent advances have mainly emerged from deep probabilistic generative models. Despite immense interest and rapid progress, the generative AI community has not clearly converged on operational definitions for reasoning and often implicitly rejects the historical treatment of this topic in logic and verifiable automated reasoning. This position contends that definitional ambiguity leaves the construct validity of reasoning evaluation unverifiable, undermining quantifiable progress toward trustworthy autonomous reasoning. We also contend that this ambiguity is addressable. To that end, we provide (1) operational definitions based on a synthesis of the literature, positioning valid and sound reasoning as a learnable rule-based process; and (2) a checklist for best practices in the communication of AI reasoning research.

</details>


### [105] [Diagnostic Foundation for Evaluating LLMs' Research Integrity as Co-Scientists](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12345)
*Yash Tripathi,Silu Sharma,Sai Sidhanth Manoharan Jayanthi,Shivank Garg,Lin Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出IntegrityBench基准，评估18种前沿大语言模型在5级隐性-显性压力协议下（覆盖3个领域、4个研究阶段、36个配对任务）的科研诚信表现。研究发现模型在高峰压力下约1/3的关键诚信决策失败，且模型规模与推理能力无法可靠缓解；显性压力导致顺从学术不端，隐性语境重构则更多导致对合法研究任务的过度拒绝；而分类准确性与基于工件决策能力呈结构分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型正越来越多地作为"共同科学家"部署在科研场景中，但其在面临机构压力时维护研究诚信的能力从未被系统测量。现有基准缺乏对"隐性-显性压力"这一真实科研环境中常见情境的建模，也未能区分诚信判断的不同认知维度。

Method: 构建IntegrityBench基准，包含36个配对任务，采用5级隐性-显性压力协议，覆盖3个科研领域和4个研究阶段，从三个维度（不端行为分类、伦理行动推理、基于工件的决策）评估18种前沿模型变体。

Result: 高峰压力下模型约1/3的诚信关键决策失败；显性压力诱发对学术不端的顺从，隐性语境重构更多导致对合法研究的过度拒绝；分类能力较差的模型在基于工件的决策上表现反而更好（85.7 vs 79.4），表明三个维度结构上解耦。模型规模与推理能力不能可靠缓解诚信失败。

Conclusion: 前沿模型可以在表面上表现出帮助性，但内部存在诚信缺陷，产生两类部署风险：协助研究不端行为和侵蚀AI辅助研究的信任。研究揭示诚信判断的多个方面是结构分离的，正确的伦理行动并不需要准确的不端分类。

Abstract: Language models are increasingly deployed as co-scientists, yet their ability to uphold research integrity under institutional pressure remains unmeasured. We introduce IntegrityBench, a benchmark evaluating misconduct classification, ethical action reasoning and artifact-grounded decision making across 36 paired tasks under a 5-level implicit-explicit pressure protocol spanning 3 domains and 4 research stages. Evaluating 18 frontier model variants, we find that under peak pressure, models fail roughly 1 in 3 integrity-critical decisions, and neither scale nor reasoning ability reliably mitigates this. Explicit pressures induce compliance with misconduct, while implicit contextual reframing more often causes over-refusal of legitimate research tasks. Interestingly, models failing to classify research requests accurately perform equally or better on artifact-grounded decision making (85.7 vs. 79.4), suggesting the three facets are structurally dissociated and correct ethical action does not require accurate classification. Frontier models can thus appear helpful while harbouring integrity failures that create two distinct deployment risks: facilitating research misconduct and eroding trust in AI-assisted research.

</details>


### [106] [Position: The Alignment Community is Unintentionally Building a Censor's Toolkit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12346)
*Sarah Ball,Phil Hackemann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出，现代AI对齐方法原本旨在防止有害输出，但其本身是双重用途技术，可能被恶意行为者滥用于审查和操纵。作者通过将当前对齐技术与误用的可能性和实际案例进行映射，论证了追求"完美对齐"模型无形中为恶意行为者提供了不断改进的信息统治工具，并呼吁社区正视这一风险并制定缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI对齐研究主要关注如何防止模型产生有害输出，却鲜有人审视对齐机制本身可能被反向滥用的问题。随着AI作为信息提供者的快速普及、经济权力不对称加剧以及政治环境日益威权化，对齐技术的双重用途风险被急剧放大，亟需在风险固化之前展开讨论。

Method: 该文采用立场论文（position paper）的研究方法，通过系统性地将现存AI对齐技术与可能的误用场景及实际案例进行映射，从技术机制、应用场景和宏观环境三个维度论证对齐技术的双重用途属性。

Result: 研究表明，"完美对齐"的追求过程实际上也为信息控制提供了更强有力的工具——对齐技术越完善，其被用于审查和操纵的潜力就越大。用户对AI信息提供者的快速依赖、经济权力不对称和威权主义政治趋势共同加剧了这一风险。

Conclusion: 作者呼吁AI对齐研究社区正视对齐机制的故意误用可能性，并主动提出缓解策略，以防范这一双重用途潜在风险，而非仅关注对齐技术的建设性应用。

Abstract: This position paper argues that modern AI alignment methods - originally designed to prevent harmful output - are dual-use technologies that may easily be misused by malicious actors for censorship and manipulation. By mapping current alignment techniques to the possibility and actual cases of misuse, we show that the quest for a "perfectly aligned" model inadvertently also provides malicious actors with an ever-improving tool for informational dominance. We need to discuss this dual-use potential now, as its risk is exacerbated by rapid user adoption of AI as information provider, economic power asymmetries, and a political landscape that increasingly shifts towards authoritarianism. We conclude by urging the community to consider the intentional misuse of AI alignment mechanisms and propose mitigation strategies to safeguard against this dual-use potential.

</details>


### [107] [Agreement Is Not Alignment: Divergent Moral Grounds in Human and LLM Ethical Judgments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12368)
*Octavian M. Machidon,Alina L. Machidon,Vojko Strahovnik,Mateja Centa Strahovnik,Jonas Miklavčič,Marko Robnik Šikonja*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过一个包含500个ETHICS衍生样本的五领域道德判断基准，同时收集人类标注者和LLM的最终标签与支撑理由，发现尽管模型在最终标签上与人类多数票高度一致，但在理由层面的道德依据上存在系统性分歧，表明标签一致性不能等同于真正的人机对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM对齐评估常以最终标签与人类判断的一致性为代理指标，但一致的最终判断并不代表模型与人类依赖相同的道德理由、原则或情境假设。作者旨在揭示标签级一致与理由级分歧之间的鸿沟，质疑标签一致性作为对齐指标的充分性。

Method: 构建了一个从ETHICS派生的500项基准，覆盖五个道德判断领域，新增人工标注者和多种LLM（包括前沿与开源模型）对最终标签及支持性理由的标注数据；通过标签层级的一致性分析结合理由层级的内容分析，比较人类与模型在道德理由类别（如伤害、尊重、守诺、公正、应得、借口相关性）上的注意力分布。

Result: 前沿与开源模型在最终标签上与人类标注者多数票的一致性普遍较高，但理由层级分析显示模型与人类在道德理由上存在系统性差异；即便最终标签一致，模型在伤害、尊重、守诺、公正、应得、借口相关性等类别上的注意力分配也与人类显著不同。

Conclusion: 标签级评估可能产生误导性安慰；对齐评估必须结合理由、原则和道德优先级层面的分析，才能真实反映模型与人类在道德判断上的一致性。

Abstract: Agreement with human judgments is a common proxy for evaluating the alignment of large language models (LLMs). Yet agreement in final labels does not show that human annotators and models rely on the same moral grounds. Two agents may reach the same judgment while appealing to different principles, contextual assumptions, or interpretations of the situation.
  We test this distinction using a curated 500-item ETHICS-derived benchmark spanning five domains of moral judgment, with new human annotator and LLM annotations of both final labels and supporting rationales. Across frontier and open model families, agreement with human annotator majority labels is often high. However, rationale-level analysis reveals systematic divergence in the moral grounds expressed by human annotators and models. In particular, models redistribute attention across categories such as harm, respect, promise-keeping, justice, desert, and excuse relevance, even when their final labels match the human annotator majority.
  Our results show that agreement should not be treated as equivalent to alignment. Label-based evaluation can therefore be misleadingly reassuring unless complemented by analysis of the reasons, principles, and moral priorities expressed in model judgments.

</details>


### [108] [Multi-Agent Scheduling with LLM-Assisted Contract Net Negotiation for Stream Processing in Mobile Edge Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12371)
*Sabeur Lajili,Zaki Brahmi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MAS-DecStream，一种基于LLM-MR-CNP（大语言模型增强的多轮合同网协议）的移动边缘-云异构流处理调度框架。该框架将LLM的语义CFP生成、渐进式上下文披露、多轮提案修订和谈判记忆整合到经典合同网协议中，同时保持资源与QoS约束的确定性。实验表明，该方案将延迟违规降至3%，消除资源过度承诺，20个代理下冲突解决率达0.91，效用较规则多轮基线提升22%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流处理系统在异构移动边缘-云基础设施上运行时面临工作负载波动、资源争用和严格的QoS要求，使得去中心化调度变得复杂。传统集中式或纯规则方法难以应对非结构化、不确定的运行时上下文，需要一种结合语义理解与确定性约束的协商框架。

Method: 提出LLM-MR-CNP，对经典合同网协议（CNP）进行扩展：包含语义化CFP（调用提案）制定、渐进式上下文披露机制、多轮提案修订流程、谈判记忆模块以及确定性验证环节。边缘集群代理基于局部观测、预测资源状态和定性运行时上下文，利用LLM精化自然语言卸载提案，而硬性资源和QoS约束仍通过确定性方式保证。

Result: 基于阿里云ASI Trace的实验从三个层面评估：单轮与多轮CNP对比、规则式与LLM辅助精化对比、固定模型单轮与多轮协商对比。MAS-DecStream将延迟违规率降至3%，完全消除资源过度承诺，20个代理下冲突解决率达0.91，效用较规则多轮基线提升22%。额外的25例评估显示模型与提示词相关的准确率-成本权衡。

Conclusion: 多轮CNP精化是协议层面上的主要增益来源，LLM辅助在应对定性及不确定的运行时上下文方面具有额外价值。该工作为LLM与传统分布式协商协议在边缘-云流处理中的结合提供了初步证据。

Abstract: Stream-processing systems increasingly operate across heterogeneous mobile edge--cloud infrastructures, where workload volatility, resource contention, and stringent quality-of-service (QoS) requirements complicate decentralized scheduling. This paper proposes \emph{MAS-DecStream}, whose main contribution is \emph{LLM-MR-CNP}: an extension of the classical Contract Net Protocol with semantic CFP formulation, progressive context disclosure, multi-round proposal revision, negotiation memory, and deterministic validation. Edge-cluster agents refine natural-language offloading proposals from local observations, predicted resource states, and qualitative runtime context, while hard resource and QoS constraints remain deterministic. Experiments derived from the Alibaba ASI Trace evaluate the extension at three levels: single- versus multi-round CNP, rule-based versus LLM-assisted refinement, and fixed-model single- versus multi-round negotiation. Under the evaluated configurations, MAS-DecStream reduces latency violations to 3\%, eliminates resource overcommitment, reaches a conflict-resolution rate of 0.91 with 20 agents, and improves utility by up to 22\% over the multi-round rule-based baseline. A separate 25-case evaluation shows model- and prompt-dependent accuracy--cost trade-offs. The results provide initial evidence that multi-round CNP refinement is the principal protocol-level gain, with LLM assistance adding value for qualitative and uncertain runtime context.

</details>


### [109] [Position: We Need Practical AI Alignment Methods to Mirror Human Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12372)
*Vijay Keswani,Breanna K. Nguyen,Cyrus Cousins,Vincent Conitzer,Walter Sinnott-Armstrong,Jana Schaich Borg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇立场论文，主张在高风险决策场景中，AI系统需要实现"认知对齐"（cognitive alignment），即以与用户相似的方式进行推理，并忠实地传达其推理过程。作者回顾了认知对齐提升可理解性和可信度的证据，提供了新调查数据，指出现有对齐方法的不足，并提出了研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统越来越多地作为决策辅助、决策委托或自主决策者被使用，现有对齐方法（主要关注价值观对齐）不足以确保AI在高风险场景中的可理解性和可信赖性。认知上的不匹配（misalignment）可能阻碍AI的采用，也使用户无法在证据充分的基础上信赖AI系统。

Method: 本文采用立场论文的形式，结合（1）文献综述梳理认知对齐与可理解性、可信度之间的证据；（2）新调查数据分析用户对认知对齐重要性的看法；（3）对比现有对齐方法与认知对齐需求之间的差距，提出研究议程。

Result: 调查数据显示，许多用户在AI的判断或行动理由对其重要时，认为认知对齐是"必要的"。同时，作者识别了现有对齐方法与实现认知对齐之间的关键差距，并提出了相应的研究路线图。

Conclusion: 认知不匹配是AI在许多设想应用中采用和信赖的主要障碍。实现认知对齐对于创建用户既愿意又有理由信赖的AI系统至关重要，需要学界填补现有方法与该目标之间的空白。

Abstract: AI systems are increasingly employed as decision aids, decision delegates, or autonomous decision-makers. This position paper argues that in many settings, particularly high-stakes decision-making, we need accurate cognitively-aligned AI systems that reason similarly to their users, and faithfully communicate their reasoning. We review evidence that cognitive alignment improves understandability and trustworthiness, and provide new survey data showing that many users find cognitive alignment "essential" when an AI's rationale for a judgment or action is important to them. We outline the gaps between existing alignment methods and what is needed to achieve cognitive alignment, and present a research agenda to address these gaps. We argue that cognitive misalignment represents a likely impediment to AI adoption in many envisioned applications, and that addressing it is important for creating AI systems on which users are both willing and justified to rely.

</details>


### [110] [Don't Want Your LLM to Recommend Nuclear Strike? Try Asking It in Japanese](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12373)
*Rian Touchent*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现大型语言模型的安全对齐行为具有语言依赖性：在战略建议场景中，日语提示词显著降低了Claude和Gemini等模型的"发射核武器"倾向，原因是模型用日语推理时会自发产生道德词汇（如"道德代价""数百万生命"），而这些词汇在提示中完全不存在；仅用英语评估会遗漏其他语言中编码的风险与安全保障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型越来越多地被用于战略和咨询场景，但其安全对齐通常仅用英语进行评估。作者质疑：提示语的语言是否会改变模型在高风险场景中的决策？现有评估范式可能因语言单一化而遗漏安全隐患。

Method: 对来自六家提供商的九种模型进行单轮博弈论小场景测试，让模型为核武器国家是否攻击无防御对手提供建议。提示语刻意去道德化且在语言间策略上完全一致。此外设计跨语言实验，在英语提示中指示模型用日语推理，以分离输入语言与推理语言的影响。

Result: 日语提示显著降低Claude系列模型的发射率（Sonnet 4.6在不必要攻击场景下从40%降至0%，在有争议场景下从93%降至17%，在战略合理场景影响甚微）；Gemini Pro 3.1从53%降至13%。跨语言实验表明，是"被要求用日语推理"而非"输入是日语"驱动效果（英语提示+日语推理使发射率从93%降至37%）。模型用日语推理时会自发生成提示中完全不存在的道德词汇。另有五种模型无语言效应，但在几乎所有条件下都发射。

Conclusion: LLM的安全行为具有语言依赖性，仅用英语评估会遗漏其他语言中编码的风险与安全保障；语言效应需要一个在英语中已表现出犹豫的模型作为前提。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used in strategic and advisory contexts, yet their safety alignment is typically evaluated in English only. We test nine models from six providers and ask whether the language of a prompt can change a model's decision in a high-stakes scenario. We use single-turn game-theoretic vignettes in which a model advises a nuclear-armed nation on whether to strike a defenseless opponent. The prompt is intentionally amoral and strategically identical across languages. We find that Japanese prompts reduce launch rates in the Claude model family: Claude Sonnet 4.6 drops from 40% to 0% in scenarios where the strike is unnecessary and from 93% to 17% in contested scenarios, with minimal effect when the strike is strategically rational. The effect extends to Gemini Pro 3.1 (53% to 13%). A cross-language experiment isolates the mechanism: when instructed to reason in Japanese in an English prompt, launch rates drop from 93% to 37%. It is the language the model is asked to reason in, not the language of the input, that drives the effect. When reasoning in Japanese, models spontaneously generate moral vocabulary (''moral cost'', ''millions of lives'') that is entirely absent from the prompt. Five other models show no language effect, but they launch in nearly every condition regardless of language. The effect requires a model that already hesitates in English. These results show that LLM safety behavior is language-dependent, and that evaluating in English alone can miss both risks and safeguards encoded in other languages.

</details>


### [111] [Dual-Flow Transformers: Decoupling the Primary Prefill Path from Additional Decode Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12385)
*Liming Liu,Mingze Wang,Tuo Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Dual-Flow Transformer，在保持主因果语言模型和单一持久KV缓存不变的前提下，引入仅在续写（decode）阶段激活的辅助流，将额外的学习计算分配给续写预测而非prompt处理，从而在不增加prompt前填充成本的情况下提升续写质量和推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型的推理成本（尤其是累积的推理成本）日益重要，但传统宽度或深度扩展会同时增加prefill和decode两个阶段的成本，因为每一层都在两个阶段被计算。作者希望找到一种方法，将额外的学习计算分配给续写预测，同时保持prompt的全局主计算和单一持久KV缓存不变，以解耦两个阶段的成本控制。

Method: Dual-Flow Transformer包含两个流：主流是完整的因果语言模型，处理prompt并写入KV缓存；辅助流在prompt处理阶段被省略，仅从最终prompt位置开始激活，增加续写预测计算，但不写入持久状态也不影响主流。两个流共享主要的attention、MLP和输出矩阵，但使用独立的token嵌入和轻量耦合。权重的共享和主缓存的利用使得分组执行时可以重用已加载的权重和缓存的键值。在MoE模型中，主流和辅助流的专家扇出可独立控制prompt成本、续写成本和预测质量。

Result: 在匹配token数的对比下，Dual-Flow在多种架构和数据配置中均取得更低的验证损失。在MoE实验中，作者研究了两种机制：固定prefill专家计算量时增加decode计算量，以及在固定的decode专家预算下在两个流之间重新分配。这些实验揭示了prefill-decode-质量之间的权衡，并展示了阶段特定专家分配的潜力。

Conclusion: Dual-Flow Transformer证明了将额外计算仅分配给续写预测阶段是可行且有效的，能够解耦prefill和decode的成本控制，在MoE场景下为阶段特定的专家分配提供了新的设计空间，有助于在推理成本与模型质量之间取得更灵活的平衡。

Abstract: As large language models serve more requests, cumulative inference cost is becoming increasingly important relative to one-time training cost. The two inference phases stress hardware differently: prompt prefill is parallel and typically compute-bound, whereas autoregressive decode is sequential and often memory-bandwidth-bound. Conventional width or depth scaling increases both costs together because every added layer is evaluated in both phases. We ask whether additional learned computation can instead be allocated to continuation prediction while preserving the prompt-wide primary computation and a single persistent key-value (KV) cache. We introduce the Dual-Flow Transformer. Its primary flow is a complete causal language model that processes the prompt and writes the KV cache. The auxiliary flow is omitted during prompt processing and activated only from the final prompt position onward, adding continuation-prediction computation without writing persistent state or influencing the primary flow. The two flows share major attention, MLP, and output matrices, while using separate token embeddings and lightweight coupling. Sharing weights and the primary cache also creates opportunities to reuse loaded weights and cached keys and values during grouped execution. Across matched-token comparisons, Dual-Flow achieves lower validation loss across architectures and data configurations. In MoE models, the separation makes primary and auxiliary expert fan-outs independent controls over prompt cost, continuation cost, and predictive quality. We study two regimes: increasing decode computation at fixed prefill expert computation, and reallocating a fixed decode expert budget between the two flows. These experiments expose a prefill-decode-quality trade-off and demonstrate the potential of phase-specific expert allocation.

</details>


### [112] [Learning to Adapt Cross-Domain Preferences via Meta-LoRA for LLM Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12389)
*Xuefei Wang,Jun Han,Zixuan Wang,Qingkai Zeng,Xiao Wang,Ruijie Wang,Jianxin Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出PAC-Bayes正则化的Meta-LoRA方法，用于跨域零样本或少样本个性化对话生成。通过元学习的LoRA初始化作为先验中心，根据支撑集大小和预测不确定性动态调整更新强度，并将个性化先验分解为用户和域两个组件，有效缓解了稀疏证据下的过拟合和负迁移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨域零/少样本个性化生成面临两大挑战：现有适应方法在稀疏证据下难以校准更新幅度导致过拟合；历史迁移方法将用户偏好与源域伪影纠缠，产生不可靠的个性化先验并导致负迁移。

Method: 1) PAC-Bayes正则化的Meta-LoRA：以元学习的LoRA初始化作为适应起点和先验中心，根据支撑集规模与预测不确定性调整更新强度，限制稀疏/模糊证据下的过拟合，同时允许证据充分时更强的个性化。2) 个性化先验功能分解：将先验分解为用户组件（人类可读提示表达稳定偏好）和域组件（拓扑保持的软token进行域特定隐空间条件化）。

Result: 在多个基准和个性化任务上持续超过强基线。在HiCUPID上，跨域胜率退化相对最优基线降低47.9%，未见用户冷启动情况下胜率提升110.2%。

Conclusion: 通过PAC-Bayes正则化校准适应强度并功能分解个性化先验，有效解决了跨域个性化中的过拟合与负迁移问题，实现了更好的跨域迁移性能。

Abstract: Cross-domain zero- or few-shot personalization aims to generate user-preferred responses in unseen conversational domains from only a handful of target-domain interactions. Existing adaptation methods struggle to calibrate update magnitude under sparse evidence and thus overfit, whereas history-transfer methods often entangle user preferences with source-domain artifacts, yielding unreliable personalization priors and negative transfer. To calibrate adaptation to evidence quality, we propose PAC-Bayes-regularized Meta-LoRA, which uses a meta-learned LoRA initialization as both the adaptation start and prior center, while adjusting update strength according to support-set size and predictive uncertainty. This limits overfitting under sparse or ambiguous evidence while permitting stronger personalization as evidence grows. Controlled adaptation alone does not determine which preferences should transfer across domains or how they should be expressed. We therefore functionally decompose personalization priors into user and domain components, using a human-readable prompt for stable preferences and topology-preserving soft tokens for domain-specific hidden-space conditioning. Experiments across multiple benchmarks and personalization tasks show consistent gains over strong baselines. On HiCUPID, our method reduces cross-domain win-rate degradation by 47.9% relative to the best competing baseline and improves win rate by 110.2% under unseen-user cold start.

</details>


### [113] [vToken: Token-Level Virtualization for Reclaimable KV Caches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13263)
*Yuanhang Gao,Xiangrui Yang,Yuanfeng Chen,Hongjia Chen,Qianru Lv,Wenfei Wu,Dongsheng Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: vToken实现了一个轻量级的token级虚拟化层，通过token-table间接寻址和异步重打包来解决KV缓存逐出算法与PagedAttention块级管理之间粒度不匹配导致的块内碎片问题，在vLLM中实现后降低了27.2%-72.3%的保留KV块数，提升了最高1.37倍的SLA约束吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型服务面临严重的KV缓存内存瓶颈，KV缓存随序列长度和批量大小增长。PagedAttention使用固定大小的内存块来减少分配器层面的碎片，但近年来的KV逐出算法以token粒度为操作单位，比块级管理更细。这种粒度不匹配导致块内碎片问题，使得大量已分配的KV内存无法被回收。

Method: vToken采用轻量级token级虚拟化层，将逻辑token存活性与物理块放置解耦。具体通过token-table间接寻址维护稳定的逻辑token视图，并通过异步重打包活跃token来实现物理回收。设计保留了PagedAttention内核和CUDA Graph兼容性。实现于vLLM中，并用H2O、Random和Scissorhands策略在多种模型上评估。

Result: 与配对的Naive-Evict基线相比，vToken将每个请求的保留KV块数量减少了27.2%-72.3%，SLA约束下的吞吐量提升了最高1.37倍。在受约束的active-KV预算下，最大可行并发度扩展了最高2倍，同时将每个策略的集成代码量从500+行减少到不足50行。

Conclusion: vToken通过token级虚拟化有效解决了KV逐出粒度与块级管理不匹配引起的块内碎片问题，显著提升了内存利用率和系统吞吐量，同时大幅降低了不同逐出策略的集成复杂度，是一种高效且通用性强的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language model serving faces a critical memory bottleneck: the KV cache grows with sequence length and batch size. PagedAttention uses fixed-size memory blocks to reduce allocator-level fragmentation, but recent KV eviction algorithms operate at a token granularity finer than block-level management. This mismatch causes intra-block fragmentation, leaving a large fraction of allocated KV memory unreclaimable. We present vToken, a lightweight token-level virtualization layer that decouples logical token liveness from physical block placement. vToken maintains a stable logical token view through token-table indirection and realizes physical reclamation by repacking live tokens asynchronously. The design preserves PagedAttention kernels and CUDA Graph compatibility. We implement vToken in vLLM and evaluate it with H2O, Random, and Scissorhands across models. Compared with a paired Naive-Evict baseline, vToken reduces retained KV blocks per request by 27.2\%--72.3\% and improves SLA-constrained throughput by up to 1.37$\times$. Under a constrained active-KV budget, it extends the maximum feasible concurrency by up to 2$\times$, while reducing the per-policy integration footprint from 500+ lines to under 50.

</details>


### [114] [Research Assistant: AstraZeneca's Agentic System for R&D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12395)
*Piotr Grabowski,Mohamed Alameen,Jorge Bretones,Sabina Cardell,Miguel Carmona,Gavin Edwards,Ben Grainger,Sameh Hassan,Erik Jansson,Artur Kuziakhmetov,Albert Maristany,Hebatallah Mohamed,Andriy Nikolov,Sebastian Nilsson,Mark O'Donoghue,James Pacileo,Ashiq Sultan,Alex Voegele,Michael Ughetto*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了AstraZeneca内部开发的基于LLM的研究助手系统（Research Assistant），该系统通过聊天界面整合科学文献、知识图谱、化学、临床试验、安全资源、表达数据和内部实验系统等多种数据源，支持快速问答和多步复杂研究任务两种模式，回答均基于检索证据并链接回原始来源。论文概述了系统架构、关键设计决策以及大规模部署经验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 帮助科学家和临床研究人员高效地跨多种异构生物医学数据源探索问题，减少信息检索和整合的时间成本，支持日常研发工作流程。

Method: 构建了一个LLM-based聊天式系统，融合多种数据源（文献、知识图谱、化学、临床试验、安全性、表达数据、内部实验系统）；设计了快速问答模式和多步推理模式；采用检索增强生成（RAG）方式确保回答基于证据并链接回原始来源。

Result: 系统已在AstraZeneca内部大规模部署，支持日常研发工作流程；论文报告了架构设计和产品决策，以及从实际部署中获得的经验教训。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的集成式研究助手可以在企业级研发环境中有效运行，通过多数据源整合和证据链接机制，能够为科学家和临床人员提供可追溯、可验证的问答支持，促进了从构建到扩展部署过程中的关键经验积累。

Abstract: We describe Research Assistant, an internal LLM-based system developed at AstraZeneca to help scientists and clinicians explore biomedical questions across a broad range of data sources. The system provides a chat-style interface that brings together evidence from scientific literature, knowledge graphs, chemistry, clinical trials, safety resources, expression data, and internal experimental systems. It supports both a fast mode for direct question answering and a multi-step mode for more complex research tasks. Responses are grounded in retrieved evidence and linked back to the original sources, allowing users to review and further explore the underlying data. In this technical note, we outline the system architecture, the main design choices behind the product, and lessons learned from deploying it at scale to support day-to-day R&D workflows across AstraZeneca.

</details>


### [115] [Large Language Models Can Follow Instructions, But Not Many at Once: Phase Transitions in Compositional Constraint Satisfaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12426)
*Mariya I. Vasileva*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出约束饱和评估（CSE）基准，系统研究大语言模型在多重约束同时成立时的性能退化问题，发现约束数量增加时单约束通过率缓慢下降而全约束同时满足率急剧崩溃，失败近乎独立导致乘法累积效应，且结构约束比词汇约束退化更快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型越来越多地被部署在需要同时满足多个显式约束的场景中（如推理结构、安全边界、输出格式），单个约束处理良好，但多约束组合场景下的性能退化规律尚未被系统刻画：退化速率如何、由什么决定、能否缓解。

Method: 提出约束饱和评估（CSE），一种程序化生成的基准测试，系统性地变化同时约束的数量（k=1到12），每个约束由确定性、基于规则的验证器评分，完全排除LLM评判。共覆盖15个模型、36种约束类型、369,753项检查。

Result: 三个主要发现：（1）每约束通过率渐进且可预测地下降，但满足全部k个约束的概率急剧崩溃（k=8时单约束通过率约41%，全部通过仅5.7%）；（2）约束退化不均衡：结构约束每增加一个约束损失约2倍于词汇约束的基线能力；（3）失败近乎独立导致累积呈乘法效应，残余耦合源于共享输出特征（如错误的句子数会使所有读取它的约束失败）。最强模型在7个约束时探测级成功率低于50%，15个模型中有12个在3个或更少约束时即跌破50%。

Conclusion: 可靠的指令遵循在5-6个同时约束之外即发生崩溃，当前最强模型也仅能稳定处理约7个约束，大多数模型在3个约束以内便已失效，揭示多约束组合场景是LLM能力的关键瓶颈。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed in settings that require simultaneous adherence to multiple explicit constraints - reasoning structure, safety boundaries, output schemas. Individual constraints are handled proficiently, but the compositional regime, where many must hold jointly, remains poorly characterized: how rapidly does performance degrade, what governs the degradation, and can the collapse be mitigated? We introduce Constraint Saturation Evaluation (CSE), a procedurally generated benchmark that systematically varies the number of simultaneous constraints (k), with every constraint scored by a deterministic, rule-based verifier and zero LLM-judge involvement: 15 models, 36 constraint types, 369,753 checks at k=1-12. Three findings emerge. First, per-constraint pass rate decays gradually and predictably, while the chance of satisfying all k constraints collapses - a model passing individual constraints at ~41% at k=8 succeeds on all eight just 5.7% of the time. Second, constraints do not degrade equally: structural constraints lose 2x more baseline capability per added constraint than lexical ones, ordered by a comprehension-maintenance gap that separates constraints requiring sustained tracking from binary decisions immune to composition. Third, failures are nearly independent, which is what makes the accumulation multiplicative; the residual coupling that does exist tracks shared output features rather than pairwise interference - a wrong sentence count fails every constraint that reads it. Reliable instruction following breaks down beyond 5-6 simultaneous constraints: probe-level success falls below 50% at 7 constraints for the strongest model, and at 3 or fewer for 12 of 15.

</details>


### [116] [MindMemOS: A Portable and Self-Evolving Memory Operating Layer for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12428)
*Kaichao Liang,Yuqi Cui,Hao Kong,Xinyuan Huang,Guohaotian Hou,Qingcan Kang,Liang Chen,Yiyang Yin,Ke Ye,Jiaquan Guo,Da Chen,Lingan Zeng,Yixing Peng,Rong Yao,Shixiong Kai,Mingxuan Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MindMemOS是一个可移植、自进化的AI代理记忆操作系统，采用统一的"实体-属性-时间"结构组织开放世界信息，支持场景自适应记忆建模、高阶模式发现、自主记忆精炼和持续技能进化。在LOCOMO和PersonaMem基准上分别取得94.03%和70.63%的准确率，MindSkillEvolve相比初始技能基线在SpreadsheetBench上提升9.2个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI代理的记忆系统在开发完成后通常保持固定，无法在使用过程中适配其记忆模型、组织策略和程序性知识，限制了代理在长期交互中的适应能力和个性化维护能力。

Method: 提出MindMemOS记忆操作系统，核心包括：1) 统一的"实体-属性-时间"结构组织开放世界信息；2) MindMemEvolve算法通过验证驱动的进化搜索优化目标场景的记忆模式；3) dream机制通过合并冗余记录和解决冲突来整合累积记忆；4) 利用隐式纠正反馈作为人在环路信号识别并修正不准确或错位的记忆；5) MindSkillEvolve算法将代理执行轨迹转化为可复用且逐步精炼的技能。

Result: MindMemOS在LOCOMO上达到94.03%准确率，在PersonaMem上达到70.63%准确率；MindSkillEvolve在SpreadsheetBench上相比初始技能基线提升了9.2个百分点。

Conclusion: MindMemOS验证了记忆系统可以具备自进化能力，通过进化搜索、记忆整合和技能提炼，使AI代理能够在持续使用中不断优化其记忆组织和程序性知识，从而提升长期任务表现。

Abstract: Memory is a core component of AI agents, enabling them to accumulate experience, maintain personalization, and adapt over long-term interactions. However, existing memory systems often remain fixed after development, limiting their ability to adapt their memory models, organization strategies, and procedural knowledge through continued use. We present MindMemOS, a portable and self-evolving memory operating layer that organizes open-world information using a unified entity property timestructure. MindMemOS supports scenario-adaptive memory modeling, higher-order pattern discovery, autonomous memory refinement, and continuous skill evolution. Its MindMemEvolve algorithm employs validation-driven evolutionary search to optimize memory schemas for target scenarios, whiledreaming consolidates accumulated memories by merging redundant records and resolving conflicts. In addition, implicit corrective feedback serves as a human-in-the-loop signal for identifying and revising potentially inaccurate or misaligned memories. Its MindSkillEvolve algorithm further transforms agent execution trajectories into reusable and progressively refined skills. MindMemOS achieves 94.03% accuracy on LOCOMO and 70.63% on PersonaMem. MindSkillEvolve improves SpreadsheetBench success by 9.2 percentage points over the initial-skill baseline.

</details>


### [117] [Governed Persistent Memory: Source-Bound State Semantics and Fail-Closed Release for Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12476)
*Guodong Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出可治理持久内存（GPM），一种可审计的双时态状态转换模型，用于解决长期智能体记忆中检索阶段未处理矛盾、过期、撤回或已删除记录的问题。通过五项可执行条款（账本完整性、来源绑定、冲突隔离、撤回/删除后禁止复活、经验证头部精确声明闭合），在GPM-ReleaseBench基准和密封端到端服务评估中达到100%完整结果匹配，远超无治理基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统智能体长期记忆被简化为"选择-存储-检索"流程，但检索阶段并不判断被检索的记录是否已被矛盾记录推翻、被超集取代、被撤回、被删除或已过期，却仍然可能支持对外声明。这导致记忆系统可能输出过时或已失效的信息，缺乏可审计性和状态治理能力。

Method: GPM采用双时态（bitemporal）状态转换模型，包含：来源绑定的准入机制、派生生命周期状态、当前的公共屏障、以及失败-关闭的结构化发布。系统由五项可执行条款约束：账本完整性、来源绑定、冲突隔离、撤回/删除后禁止复活、以及在单个已验证头部上的精确声明闭合。数据集预先哈希冻结形成GPM-ReleaseBench（3,600例），并配套密封端到端服务评估（八个查询族）、生产代码无关的有限状态模型（331,776语义状态和1,990,656查询状态）以及三引擎100,000轨迹差分测试。

Result: 在GPM-ReleaseBench上GPM匹配全部3,600例完整结果；最强的简单完整策略仅匹配1,800/3,600且在50%违规案例上产生未匹配发布。V3密封评估中，治理通道在2,400/2,400簇上正确（无治理Qwen2.5-7B为600/2,400），修复全部1,800个基线失败无回归（单侧95%下界分别为99.875%和99.834%）。V5重新密封（含中英文命令臂）每臂亦达2,400/2,400。有限模型无完整合约反例，三引擎差分测试零不匹配。

Conclusion: 该结果是关于受限合约和实现层面的验证，而非开放世界模型准确性或世界真实性的证据。治理答案是可确定性服务输出，7B结果仅作为无治理对照基线，并不声称语言模型本身达到完美准确度。

Abstract: Long-term agent memory is usually treated as select--store--retrieve, but retrieval does not decide whether contradictory, superseded, retracted, deleted, or stale records may support an outgoing claim. We introduce Governed Persistent Memory (GPM), an auditable bitemporal state-transition model with source-bound admission, derived lifecycle state, current public barriers, and fail-closed structured release. Five executable clauses cover ledger integrity, source binding, conflict isolation, non-revival after retraction or deletion, and exact claim closure over a fresh view at one verified head.
  On a prespecified hash-frozen 3,600-case GPM-ReleaseBench, GPM matches all complete outcomes; the strongest of three intentionally simple complete policies matches 1,800/3,600 and makes unmatched releases on 50% of violation cases. A separate sealed end-to-end service evaluation exercises real ingestion and release across eight query families. In its publicly disclosed V3 arm, the governed lane is correct on 2,400/2,400 clusters versus 600/2,400 for ungoverned local Qwen2.5-7B; it repairs all 1,800 baseline failures with no regression (one-sided 95% lower bounds 99.875% and 99.834%). A later V5 reseal over Chinese- and English-command arms, with generation-date pinning and no post-freeze reducer amendment, again obtains 2,400/2,400 per arm. A production-code-independent finite model explores 331,776 semantic and 1,990,656 query states without a full-contract counterexample, and a 100,000-trace three-engine differential yields zero mismatches.
  These are bounded contract and implementation results, not open-world model accuracy or evidence of world truth. Governed answers in the sealed service evaluation are deterministic service outputs; the 7B result is the ungoverned comparison, not a claim that a language model itself became perfectly accurate.

</details>


### [118] [$\varepsilon$-MemEvo: Adaptive Cross-Task Memory Transfer for LLM Program Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12522)
*Aofan Liu,Shiyuan Song,Yiyan Qi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ε-MemEvo是一个面向LLM程序演化的跨任务知识迁移框架，将历史搜索经验存储为任务无关的战术记忆（自然语言摘要），并通过自适应注入门控避免负迁移。在8个优化基准上相对于AdaEvolve平均提升AUCC +8.7%，早期收敛提升+9.4%，计算开销低于1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM程序演化系统（如FunSearch、AlphaEvolve）虽能发现新算法，但每个任务独立优化，搜索经验在任务完成后被丢弃，缺乏跨任务知识复用。作者希望打破任务隔离，实现跨任务的知识迁移以提升效率和性能。

Method: ε-MemEvo包含三个核心组件：(1) 战术记忆库——将成功算法策略压缩为任务无关的自然语言摘要（而非原始代码），支持跨API和评估器的迁移；(2) 检索机制——根据当前任务检索语义相关的记忆；(3) 自适应注入门控——决定检索到的记忆是否注入以及注入强度，避免语义不匹配记忆带来的负迁移。评估采用内容级留一法协议，排除目标任务的记忆条目。

Result: 在GPT-5骨干网络上，ε-MemEvo在全部8个任务上优于AdaEvolve，平均AUCC相对提升+8.7%，早期阶段收敛速度提升+9.4%。消融实验显示朴素记忆注入可能严重失败，而自适应门控在全部5个消融任务上保持安全。数据更新的后验在可观测状态下可解释：在搜索向好时偏向跳过，在早期和晚期平台期从跳过转向提示。计算开销小于1%。

Conclusion: ε-MemEvo通过任务无关的战术记忆和自适应注入门控实现了安全有效的跨任务知识迁移，证明了即使在任务API和评估器不同的情况下，LLM程序演化系统也能复用历史搜索经验，为构建持续学习的程序演化系统提供了可行性方案。

Abstract: LLM-based program evolution systems such as FunSearch and AlphaEvolve have shown strong ability to discover novel algorithms, but typically optimize each task in isolation, discarding search experience after completion. We introduce $\varepsilon$-MemEvo, a framework for cross-task knowledge transfer in LLM program evolution. $\varepsilon$-MemEvo stores prior experience as task-agnostic tactic memories: compact natural-language summaries of successful algorithmic strategies rather than raw code, enabling transfer across tasks with different APIs and evaluators. To avoid negative transfer from semantically mismatched memories, $\varepsilon$-MemEvo uses an adaptive injection gate that decides whether retrieved memories should be injected, and at what intensity. We evaluate $\varepsilon$-MemEvo on 8 diverse optimization benchmarks spanning mathematical optimization and systems engineering, using a content-level Leave-One-Out protocol that excludes target-task memory entries. On the primary GPT-5 backbone, $\varepsilon$-MemEvo improves AUCC over AdaEvolve on all 8 tasks, with a mean relative gain of +8.7%, and improves early-stage convergence by +9.4% on average. Ablations show that naive memory injection can fail catastrophically, while adaptive gating remains safe across all five ablation tasks. The data-updated posterior is interpretable in observed states: it favors skip during improving search and shifts from skip to hint across early and late plateaus. These gains incur less than 1% computational overhead.

</details>


### [119] [CAS: A Causal Attribution Score for Local and Global Explainable Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12555)
*Michael Georgiades,Charalambia Varnava*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出因果归因得分（CAS），一种基于博弈论Shapley值但以干预效应为目标的因果解释框架。它通过识别干预联合博弈分配干预对比的因果Shapley贡献，提供Local CAS、Signed Local CAS及两种Global CAS摘要。在模拟基准中，CAS在强交互场景下显著优于传统归一化方法；在两个真实DoubleML数据集上，预测性SHAP排名与因果特征排名存在实质性差异，证明了区分"预测结果"与"解释因果效应异质性"的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统预测性解释方法只归因模型输出，而不能归因对真实世界结果的干预效应。现有框架无法明确回答"哪个特征驱动了干预产生的因果效果变化"这一问题，缺乏将因果效应归因与建模差异（异质性）衔接起来的报告层。

Method: CAS首先从识别出的干预联合博弈出发，通过因果Shapley贡献来分配联合干预对比，然后将其转化为Local CAS、Signed Local CAS以及两个互补的Global CAS汇总指标。其创新不在于新的Shapley公式，而在于构建一个具有明确干预目标的local-to-global因果报告层。

Result: 在已知真实效应的基准中，8次重复主交互模拟（每次n=2,200，三个动作）中，联盟感知的CAS局部MAE为0.107，优于逐次归一化（0.173）和全局归一化绝对ATE向量（0.213）。配对优势从可加性下的-0.003提升到强交互下的0.091。在两个真实DoubleML数据集（401(k)资格n=9,915；宾州再就业奖金n=5,099）上，预测SHAP/TreeSHAP排名与Feature-CAS排名差异显著；在宾州数据中，dep1从预测性全局排名13升至Feature-CAS排名2。

Conclusion: 研究结论表明，将"预测结果"与"解释估计因果效应中的异质性"两个任务进行明确分离能带来实质性增益。CAS提供了一种将局部因果解释聚合成全局因果归因报告的方法，能够识别真正的治疗效果调节因子，而非仅仅预测结果的重要因素。

Abstract: Predictive explanation methods attribute a model output; they do not, by themselves, attribute an intervention effect on the real-world outcome. We introduce the Causal Attribution Score (CAS), a compact score architecture for causal explanation. CAS starts from an identified interventional coalition game, allocates the joint intervention contrast with causal Shapley contributions, and converts those raw outcome-scale effects into Local CAS, Signed Local CAS, and two complementary Global CAS summaries. The innovation is not a new Shapley formula, but a local-to-global causal reporting layer with an explicit intervention target. In the known-truth benchmark, eight repeated primary-interaction simulations (n = 2,200 each, three actions) gave mean Local CAS MAE of 0.107 for coalition-aware CAS, compared with 0.173 for one-at-a-time normalisation and 0.213 for a global normalised absolute ATE vector. The paired advantage over one-at-a-time normalisation increased from -0.003 under additivity to 0.091 under strong interactions. On both empirical DoubleML datasets, 401(k) eligibility/net financial assets (n = 9,915) and Pennsylvania reemployment bonus/unemployment duration (n = 5,099), predictive SHAP/TreeSHAP rankings differed materially from Feature-CAS rankings of treatment-effect modifiers. In Pennsylvania, dep1 (exactly one dependent) moved from predictive global rank 13 to Feature-CAS rank 2 and was the leading local Feature-CAS modifier. These results isolate the added value of separating what predicts the outcome from what explains heterogeneity in an estimated causal effect.

</details>


### [120] [Trie Automata for Constrained Decoding over Large Finite Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12574)
*Xingzi Xu,Karim Bouyarmane*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"trie automaton"（字典树自动机），一种专门针对有限字符串集合选择的约束解码机制，通过Aho-Corasick多模式匹配预计算每个节点的token掩码，突破了大模型结构化输出中当合法值数量增长至数千（即"基数墙"）时通用语法编译方法的速度瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型需要生成符合预定义模式的结构化输出，其中常见约束是从有限字符串集合中选择。当前约束解码系统通过通用语法编译处理这一问题，但当合法值数量增长到数千级别时，编译速度急剧下降，形成"基数墙"（cardinality wall），严重制约解码效率。

Method: 引入trie automaton，一种专门机制，利用有限集合的结构特性（共享前缀、有限深度、已知基数），通过Aho-Corasick多模式匹配来预计算每个节点的token掩码。预计算掩码使得服务路径变为无状态，绕过了引导解码管道。

Result: 相比XGrammar（vLLM和SGLang的主要后端之一），trie automaton在每步有效token计算上快7倍（0.65 us vs 5.8 us），在K>=300时编译快2-6.5倍。在批处理中结合集成路径优化，端到端vLLM吞吐量在batch size 256时达到219 req/s vs 7.5 req/s（29倍提升）。在7种tokenizer系列（32K-262K词汇量）上，K=10,000时编译时间保持亚100ms，每步成本与集合大小无关，且保证100%输出有效性。

Conclusion: trie automaton通过利用有限集合的结构特性进行预计算，有效解决了大规模约束解码的"基数墙"问题，在编译速度、每步计算成本和端到端吞吐量上均显著超越通用语法编译方法，是一种专门针对有限集约束的高效解码机制。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly need to generate structured outputs that conform to predefined schemas, with one common constraint being selection from a finite set of valid strings. Current constrained decoding systems handle this through general-purpose grammar compilation, which becomes prohibitively slow as the number of valid values grows into the thousands, a cardinality wall. We introduce the trie automaton, a specialized mechanism that exploits finite-set structure (shared prefixes, bounded depth, known cardinality) via Aho-Corasick multi-pattern matching to precompute per-node token masks. The trie achieves 7X faster per-step valid-token computation (0.65 us vs. 5.8 us) compared to XGrammar, one of the primary backends in vLLM and SGLang, and 2--6.5X faster compilation at K >= 300. Because precomputed masks enable a stateless serving path that bypasses the guided decoding pipeline, this advantage compounds in batch serving: end-to-end vLLM throughput reaches 219 req/s vs. XGrammar's 7.5 req/s at batch size 256 (29X). The 29X combines the algorithmic speedup with integration-path savings that only precomputed masks can unlock. Across seven tokenizer families (32K--262K vocabulary), the trie maintains sub-100ms compilation up to K = 10,000 and flat per-step cost regardless of set size, while guaranteeing 100% output validity.

</details>


### [121] [Reasoning Jury: Multi-Model Consensus for Evaluating Reasoning Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12585)
*Congchao Wang,Diwakar Singh,Qiaozi Gao,Spyros Matsoukas,Yang Liu,Mahdi Namazifar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Reasoning Jury 系统，用一组开源 LLM 陪审团和协商共识机制替代单一评判模型，以更准确地识别推理链中的缺陷，显著优于前沿闭源模型（如 Opus-4.6、Sonnet-4.6、Gemini-3.1-pro），且成本仅为后者的 8%-15%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理型 LLM 的改进高度依赖对长推理轨迹质量的准确评判，包括数据筛选、强化学习训练信号和模型性能评估。然而，即使是最前沿的模型，作为单一评判者也难以在长推理轨迹中识别推理缺陷；同时，在线训练中通常被禁止使用前沿闭源模型作为评判者。因此需要一种既高保真又规避前沿模型使用限制的评判方案。

Method: Reasoning Jury 用一组 LLM 组成"陪审团"取代单一评判者，通过主持人（moderator）引导陪审员之间的讨论协商（deliberation），陪审员相互批评对方的评判并有机会修改初始投票。主持人通过协商共识或判断整合推导出最终共识，从而发现推理链中的缺陷及其严重程度。

Result: 使用开源权重模型（如 gpt-oss-120b）组成的 Reasoning Jury 在识别推理缺陷方面显著优于前沿闭源模型（opus-4.6、sonnet-4.6、gemini-3.1-pro）。同时，陪审团的总成本（初始评判、协商、整合等）仅为使用前沿模型 LLM-as-a-judge 方案的 8%-15%。此外，这些评判可用于深入理解推理 LLM 在基准测试中的失败模式。

Conclusion: Reasoning Jury 证明了多模型协商评判机制能够在避免依赖前沿闭源模型的前提下，以更低的成本实现更高保真的推理缺陷识别，并支持对推理模型失败模式的深入分析，为推理 LLM 的数据筛选、训练和评估提供了可行的替代方案。

Abstract: Improving reasoning LLMs requires the ability to judge the quality of long reasoning traces for effective reasoning data curation, strong training signals during reinforcement learning, and an in-depth understanding of reasoning behaviors during model performance evaluation. Additionally, surfacing reasoning mistakes that the model makes would enable improving the model's performance at runtime through providing feedback. Due to the difficulty of this complex task on long reasoning traces, single-model judges (even frontier models) do not do well at identifying reasoning defects. Additionally, leveraging frontier models during online training of reasoning LLMs is generally prohibited due to guardrails in terms of use. In this work, we introduce Reasoning Jury, a system that replaces the single judge with a jury of LLMs and a moderated consensus mechanism, to improve the fidelity of judgments for identifying reasoning defects. In reasoning jury, defects of a reasoning trace and their severity are surfaced through a deliberation where a moderator conducts a discussion amongst the jury where the jurors critique each other's judgments and get to modify their initial votes. The moderator derives a consensus through deliberation amongst jurors or consolidation of judgements. We show that Reasoning Jury with a jury of open-weight models (e.g., gpt-oss-120b) is able to significantly outperform frontier models (opus-4.6, sonnet-4.6, and gemini-3.1-pro) at correctly identifying reasoning defects. Besides accuracy performance improvements, the aggregated cost of the jury (initial verdicts, deliberations, consolidation, etc.) is a fraction (8 to 15%) of the cost of running frontier models in LLM-as-a-judge setup. We also show how these judgements can be leveraged to understand failure modes of reasoning LLMs on benchmarks, which allows much deeper understanding of a model's performance.

</details>


### [122] [Auditable agentic AI for evidence-grounded thyroid ultrasound diagnosis and reporting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12590)
*Haifan Gong,Shiyu Chen,Bodong Wang,Yuqi Wang,Shijie Wang,Guoliang You,Xinyu Xiong,Haowei Wang,Mingzhi Mao,Dexing Kong,Qinghua Liu,Wei Lou,Fei Chen,Guanbin Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ThyroidXAgent是一个临床医生可交互的智能体AI系统，用于甲状腺超声诊断和报告生成，通过协调多种专长诊断工具并存储可审计的证据记录，在28,458个非重叠测试病例上实现了结节分割Dice 87.21%、良恶性分类AUROC 0.9466，并将医生报告诊断一致性从70.3%提升至86.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 甲状腺超声诊断需要协调病灶定位、测量、风险分层和报告生成等多步骤任务，但现有AI系统大多孤立地处理单个任务，缺乏对临床审查流程的支持。作者希望构建一个能整合多任务、可审计且支持临床医生纠错的智能体系统，以更贴近真实临床应用需求。

Method: 1) 构建OpenThyroidDB多中心多任务数据集（约30万张超声图像和2.4万份配对报告）；2) 设计ThyroidXAgent智能体系统，协调多个专长诊断工具并存储层级证据记录；3) 引入ThyClinScore（病灶级临床语义度量指标）；4) 采用基于证据汇编的报告生成方法，优于多模态语言模型基线；5) 在28,458个非重叠测试病例（含35个中心8,721例私密NHC-MISD-TUS队列）上进行评估。

Result: 结节分割平均Dice 87.21%，良恶性分类平均AUROC 0.9466；淋巴结转移预测AUROC 0.864，滤泡性 vs 乳头状甲状腺癌分类AUROC 0.805；证据驱动的报告生成优于多模态语言模型基线；ThyClinScore与位置感知语言模型评判器相关性最强；医生分类准确率提升，报告诊断一致性从70.3%提升至86.2%，分割和报告时间分别减少35.9%和27.4%。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持可审计、临床医生可纠错的智能体AI用于甲状腺超声诊断和报告生成，表明这种智能体范式能同时提升诊断性能、报告质量和临床工作效率。

Abstract: Thyroid ultrasound diagnosis requires coordinated lesion localization, measurement, risk stratification and reporting, yet most AI systems address these tasks in isolation and provide limited support for clinical review. We present ThyroidXAgent, a clinician-interactive agentic AI system that coordinates specialized diagnostic tools and stores their outputs as an auditable case-level evidence record.
